   260	  function _validateAuthoritativeHypothesisSet(hsIn, refNowMs, maxAgeMs) {
   261	    if (!_isPlainObject(hsIn)) {
   262	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   263	    }
   264	    if (!_isFrozenSafe(hsIn)) {
   265	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   266	    }
   267	    // Codex D4 R1 D4-R1-02 closure: top-level descriptor audit BEFORE clone — rejects
   268	    // accessor descriptors on any of hsIn's top-level fields (e.g. createdAt getter).
   269	    // structuredClone fires accessor [[Get]] traps; pre-clone rejection prevents that.
   270	    var hsInOwn = HI.safeOwnKeys(hsIn);
   271	    if (hsInOwn === null) {
   272	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   273	    }
   274	    for (var hsi = 0; hsi < hsInOwn.length; hsi++) {
   275	      var hsk = hsInOwn[hsi];
   276	      if (typeof hsk === 'symbol') {
   277	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   278	      }
   279	      var hsDesc = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(hsIn, hsk);
   280	      if (!hsDesc) continue;
   281	      if (!('value' in hsDesc)) {
   282	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   283	      }
   284	      if (!hsDesc.enumerable) {
   285	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   286	      }
   287	    }
   288	    var hs = HI.safeStructuredClone(hsIn);
   289	    if (hs === null || !_isPlainObject(hs)) {
   290	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   291	    }
   292	    if (HI.deepOriginalShapeAudit(hs) !== true) {
   293	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   294	    }
   295	    // Closed key set check on the clone.
   296	    var hsCloneKeys = HI.safeOwnKeys(hs);
   297	    if (hsCloneKeys === null) {
   298	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   299	    }
   300	    for (var hcki = 0; hcki < hsCloneKeys.length; hcki++) {
   301	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(HSET_KEYS_ALLOWED, hsCloneKeys[hcki]) === -1) {
   302	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY] };
   303	      }
   304	    }
   305	    if (hs.schemaVersion !== 1) {
   306	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA] };
   307	    }
   308	    if (!_isNonEmptyString(hs.hypothesisSetId)
   309	        || !HI.safeRegExpTest(/^hset_[0-9a-f]{16}$/, hs.hypothesisSetId)) {
   310	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   311	    }
   312	    if (!_isNonEmptyString(hs.sourceGraphId)
   313	        || !HI.safeRegExpTest(/^graph_[0-9a-f]{16}$/, hs.sourceGraphId)) {
   314	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   315	    }
   316	    if (!_isPlainObject(hs.caseAssociation)
   317	        || !_isNonEmptyString(hs.caseAssociation.caseId)
   318	        || !_isNonEmptyString(hs.caseAssociation.sessionId)) {
   319	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID] };
   320	    }
   321	    if (!_isPlainObject(hs.sessionAssociation)
   322	        || hs.sessionAssociation.sessionId !== hs.caseAssociation.sessionId) {
   323	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_SESSION_MISMATCH] };
   324	    }
   325	    if (!_isPlainArray(hs.hypotheses)) {
   326	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   327	    }
   328	    if (hs.hypotheses.length > INPUT_HYPOTHESES_INPUT_CAP) {
   329	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   330	    }
   331	    if (!_isPlainArray(hs.cannotConclude)) {
   332	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   333	    }
   334	    if (!_isPlainArray(hs.limitations)) {
   335	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   336	    }
   337	    // ---- Per-hypothesis structural + cross-field validation (Codex D4 R1 D4-R1-01/03 closure) ----
   338	    var seenHypIds = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   339	    for (var hi = 0; hi < hs.hypotheses.length; hi++) {
   340	      var h = hs.hypotheses[hi];
   341	      if (!_isPlainObject(h)) {
   342	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   343	      }
   344	      // Closed key set
   345	      var hKeys = HI.safeOwnKeys(h);
   346	      if (hKeys === null) return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   347	      for (var hki = 0; hki < hKeys.length; hki++) {
   348	        if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(HYPOTHESIS_KEYS_ALLOWED, hKeys[hki]) === -1) {
   349	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY] };
   350	        }
   351	      }
   352	      // hypothesisId grammar + uniqueness
   353	      if (!_isNonEmptyString(h.hypothesisId)
   354	          || HI.safeRegExpTest(ID_FORBIDDEN_RE, h.hypothesisId)
   355	          || !HI.safeRegExpTest(/^hyp_[0-9a-f]{16}$/, h.hypothesisId)) {
   356	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   357	      }
   358	      if (HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(seenHypIds, h.hypothesisId)) {
   359	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   360	      }
   361	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(seenHypIds, h.hypothesisId, true);
   362	      // ruleId required + must be in HE.RULE_REGISTRY
   363	      if (!_isNonEmptyString(h.ruleId)) {
   364	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   365	      }
   366	      var rDesc = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(_RULE_BY_ID, h.ruleId);
   367	      if (!rDesc) {
   368	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   369	      }
   370	      var rule = rDesc.value;
   371	      // Codex D4 R1 D4-R1-01 closure: hypothesisId recompute equality binds (ruleId,
   372	      // supportingEvidenceIds, contradictingEvidenceIds) per D3's _hypothesisId. Caller can no
   373	      // longer keep IDs while mutating those fields.
   374	      if (!_isPlainArray(h.supportingEvidenceIds)) {
   375	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_EVIDENCE_LINK_INVALID] };
   376	      }
   377	      if (!_isPlainArray(h.contradictingEvidenceIds)) {
   378	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CONTRADICTION_INVALID] };
   379	      }
   380	      // All evidence IDs must be non-empty strings + cap
   381	      if (h.supportingEvidenceIds.length > 64 || h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length > 64) {
   382	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   383	      }
   384	      for (var sei = 0; sei < h.supportingEvidenceIds.length; sei++) {
   385	        if (!_isNonEmptyString(h.supportingEvidenceIds[sei])) {
   386	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_EVIDENCE_LINK_INVALID] };
   387	        }
   388	      }
   389	      for (var cei = 0; cei < h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length; cei++) {
   390	        if (!_isNonEmptyString(h.contradictingEvidenceIds[cei])) {
   391	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CONTRADICTION_INVALID] };
   392	        }
   393	      }
   394	      var idMaterial = {
   395	        v: hs.schemaVersion,
   396	        ruleId: h.ruleId,
   397	        supportingEvidenceIds: HI.safeArraySlice(h.supportingEvidenceIds),
   398	        contradictingEvidenceIds: HI.safeArraySlice(h.contradictingEvidenceIds),
   399	      };
   400	      var expectedHid = 'hyp_' + _hashFNV64Hex('hypothesisid|v' + hs.schemaVersion + '|' + HI.stableStringify(idMaterial));
   401	      if (expectedHid !== h.hypothesisId) {
   402	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   403	      }
   404	      // Cross-field consistency with rule (Codex D4 R1 D4-R1-01 closure continued).
   405	      if (h.category !== rule.category) {
   406	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CATEGORY_UNKNOWN] };
   407	      }
   408	      if (h.i18nKey !== rule.i18nKey) {
   409	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   410	      }
   411	      // Credibility / confidence / status — closed enums
   412	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(CONCLUSION_CREDIBILITY_ORDER, h.credibility) === -1) {
   413	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   414	      }
   415	      if (!_crAtLeast(h.credibility, rule.minConclusionCredibility)) {
   416	        // Credibility weaker than rule allows → invalid (D3 wouldn't have emitted this)
   417	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   418	      }
   419	      if (!_isPlainObject(h.confidence)) {
   420	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   421	      }
   422	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(CONFIDENCE_STATE_ALLOWED, h.confidence.state) === -1) {
   423	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   424	      }
   425	      if (typeof h.confidence.score !== 'number'
   426	          || !_isInteger(h.confidence.score)
   427	          || h.confidence.score < 0
   428	          || h.confidence.score > 100) {
   429	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   430	      }
   431	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(HYPOTHESIS_STATUS_ALLOWED, h.status) === -1) {
   432	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   433	      }
   434	      // Cross-field: status='contradicted' ⟹ contradictingEvidenceIds.length > 0
   435	      if (h.status === 'contradicted' && h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length === 0) {
   436	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   437	      }
   438	      // status='supported' ⟹ supportingEvidenceIds.length > 0
   439	      if (h.status === 'supported' && h.supportingEvidenceIds.length === 0) {
   440	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   441	      }
   442	      if (!_isPlainArray(h.limitations)) {
   443	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   444	      }
   445	      if (h.limitations.length > 32) {
   446	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   447	      }
   448	      for (var lhi = 0; lhi < h.limitations.length; lhi++) {
   449	        if (!RC.isReasonCode(h.limitations[lhi])) {
   450	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   451	        }
   452	      }
   453	    }
   454	
   455	    // Recompute hypothesisSetId — independent authority verification.
   490	   *   - status NOT 'contradicted' or 'blocked'
   491	   *   - confidence.state ∈ {moderate, high}
   492	   * setup_experiment additionally requires: zero contradicting evidence + confidence high
   493	   * (per directive: "unsupported setup actions" forbidden).
   494	   */
   495	  function _eligibleAsPrimary(h) {
   496	    if (!_crAtLeast(h.credibility, 'Derived')) return false;
   497	    if (h.status === 'contradicted' || h.status === 'blocked') return false;
   498	    if (h.confidence.state !== 'moderate' && h.confidence.state !== 'high') return false;
   499	    return true;
   500	  }
   501	  function _eligibleAsSetupExperiment(h) {
   502	    if (h.contradictingEvidenceIds && h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length > 0) return false;
   503	    if (h.confidence.state !== 'high') return false;
   504	    if (!_crAtLeast(h.credibility, 'Derived')) return false;
   505	    return true;
   506	  }
   507	
   508	  // ---------- Priority ID generators -----------------------------------------------------------
   509	  function _priorityId(hypothesisId, kind, schemaVersion) {
   510	    var hash = _hashFNV64Hex('priorityid|v' + schemaVersion + '|' + hypothesisId + '|' + kind);
   511	    return 'pri_' + hash;
   512	  }
   513	  function _prioritySetId(sourceHsetId, priorities, schemaVersion) {
   514	    var hashMaterial = {
   515	      v: schemaVersion,
   516	      hsetId: sourceHsetId,
   517	      priIds: HI.safeArrayMap(priorities, function (p) { return p.priorityId; }),
   518	    };
   519	    HI.safeArraySort(hashMaterial.priIds, _strcmp);
   520	    return 'priset_' + _hashFNV64Hex('prioritysetid|v' + schemaVersion + '|' + HI.stableStringify(hashMaterial));
   521	  }
   522	
   523	  // ---------- Build one priority entry ---------------------------------------------------------
   524	  function _buildPriorityEntry(h, tier, blockingIds, schemaVersion) {
   525	    var kind = _kindForCategory(h.category);
   526	    // For setup_experiment, additional eligibility gate
   527	    if (kind === 'setup_experiment' && !_eligibleAsSetupExperiment(h)) {
   528	      // Downgrade to controlled_repeat_lap (still actionable, but doesn't claim setup causation)
   529	      kind = 'controlled_repeat_lap';
   530	      tier = PRIORITY_KIND_TIER[kind];
   531	    }
   532	    if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(PRIORITY_KIND_ALLOWED, kind) === -1) {
   533	      kind = 'no_action_required';
   534	      tier = PRIORITY_KIND_TIER[kind];
   535	    }
   536	    var pid = _priorityId(h.hypothesisId, kind, schemaVersion);
   537	
   538	    // Limitations (carry forward from hypothesis + add a default if heuristic / contradicted)
   539	    var lims = HI.safeArraySlice(_isPlainArray(h.limitations) ? h.limitations : []);
   540	    if (!_crAtLeast(h.credibility, 'Derived')
   541	        && HI.safeArrayIndexOf(lims, CODES.LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY) === -1) {
   542	      _arrPush(lims, CODES.LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY);
   543	    }
   544	    if (h.contradictingEvidenceIds && h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length > 0
   545	        && HI.safeArrayIndexOf(lims, CODES.LIMITATION_NO_CONTROLLED_REPEAT) === -1) {
   546	      _arrPush(lims, CODES.LIMITATION_NO_CONTROLLED_REPEAT);
   547	    }
   548	    HI.safeArraySort(lims, _strcmp);
   549	    if (lims.length > LIMITATIONS_PER_PRIORITY_CAP) {
   550	      var trimmed = [];
   551	      for (var li = 0; li < LIMITATIONS_PER_PRIORITY_CAP; li++) _arrPush(trimmed, lims[li]);
   552	      lims = trimmed;
   553	    }
   554	
   555	    var sortedBlocking = HI.safeArraySlice(blockingIds);
   556	    HI.safeArraySort(sortedBlocking, _strcmp);
   557	    if (sortedBlocking.length > PREREQ_ID_PER_PRIORITY_CAP) {
   558	      var blkTrimmed = [];
   559	      for (var bi = 0; bi < PREREQ_ID_PER_PRIORITY_CAP; bi++) _arrPush(blkTrimmed, sortedBlocking[bi]);
   560	      sortedBlocking = blkTrimmed;
   561	    }
   562	
   563	    // i18nKey for the priority itself, sourced deterministically from the kind.
   564	    var i18nKey = 'r3.0d.priority.' + kind;
   565	    var whyNowI18nKey = 'r3.0d.priority.why_now.' + kind;
   566	    var expectedObsKey = 'r3.0d.priority.expected.' + kind;
   567	    var stopKey = 'r3.0d.priority.stop.' + kind;
   568	    var rollbackKey = 'r3.0d.priority.rollback.' + kind;
   569	
   570	    return HI.deepFreeze({
   571	      priorityId: pid,
   572	      hypothesisId: h.hypothesisId,
   573	      rank: 0,  // assigned post-sort
   574	      tier: tier,
   575	      kind: kind,
   576	      category: h.category,
   577	      i18nKey: i18nKey,
   578	      whyNowI18nKey: whyNowI18nKey,
   579	      blockingPrerequisiteIds: HI.deepFreeze(sortedBlocking),
   580	      expectedObservationI18nKey: expectedObsKey,
   581	      stopConditionI18nKey: stopKey,
   582	      rollbackConditionI18nKey: rollbackKey,
   583	      credibility: h.credibility,
   584	      confidence: HI.deepFreeze({ state: h.confidence.state, score: h.confidence.score }),
   585	      eligibleAsPrimary: _eligibleAsPrimary(h),
   586	      contradictionCount: h.contradictingEvidenceIds ? h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length : 0,
   587	      limitations: HI.deepFreeze(lims),
   588	      provenance: HI.deepFreeze({
   589	        sourceHypothesisId: h.hypothesisId,
   590	        sourceCategory: h.category,
   591	        sourceStatus: h.status,
   592	        builderVersion: SERVICE_VERSION,
   593	      }),
   594	    });
   595	  }
   596	
   597	  // ---------- Public entry: buildPrioritySet ---------------------------------------------------
   598	  function buildPrioritySet(inputIn, optsIn) {
   599	    try {
   600	      if (!_intrinsicsIntact()) {
   601	        return _buildIntrinsicTamperBlock('intrinsic-tampering detected at entry to buildPrioritySet');
   602	      }
   603	
   604	      // ---- Step 0.5 — input + opts descriptor snapshot ----
   605	      if (!_isPlainObject(inputIn)) {
   606	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'input not plain object' });
   607	      }
   608	      var inputSnap = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   609	      var inputDescNames = HI.safeOwnKeys(inputIn);
   610	      if (inputDescNames === null) {
   750	        var cv = inputSnap.contextVersion;
   751	        if (cv !== null) {
   752	          if (!_isNonEmptyString(cv) || cv.length > 128) {
   753	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'contextVersion must be non-empty string ≤128 chars or null' });
   754	          }
   755	          contextVersion = cv;
   756	        }
   757	      }
   758	
   759	      // ---- Step 3 — finalize kind + tier + priorityId for each hypothesis (PASS 1) ----
   760	      // Codex D4 R1 D4-R1-04 closure: apply downgrade FIRST (e.g. setup_experiment that fails
   761	      // eligibleAsSetupExperiment → controlled_repeat_lap), THEN compute blocking prerequisites
   762	      // against the FINAL priorityIds. Previously preliminary IDs reflected the pre-downgrade
   763	      // kind, so blockingPrerequisiteIds could reference IDs absent from the final set.
   764	      var finalized = [];
   765	      for (var hi2 = 0; hi2 < hs.hypotheses.length; hi2++) {
   766	        var h2 = hs.hypotheses[hi2];
   767	        if (h2.status === 'blocked') continue;
   768	        var fkind = _kindForCategory(h2.category);
   769	        if (fkind === 'setup_experiment' && !_eligibleAsSetupExperiment(h2)) {
   770	          fkind = 'controlled_repeat_lap';
   771	        }
   772	        if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(PRIORITY_KIND_ALLOWED, fkind) === -1) {
   773	          fkind = 'no_action_required';
   774	        }
   775	        var ftier = PRIORITY_KIND_TIER[fkind];
   776	        if (typeof ftier !== 'number') continue;
   777	        var fpid = _priorityId(h2.hypothesisId, fkind, PRIORITY_SET_SCHEMA_VERSION);
   778	        _arrPush(finalized, { hyp: h2, kind: fkind, tier: ftier, priorityId: fpid });
   779	      }
   780	
   781	      // ---- Step 4 — compute blocking prerequisites per FINAL priorityId (PASS 2) ----
   782	      // Verify referential integrity: every blockingPrerequisiteId MUST exist in finalized[].
   783	      var finalIdSet = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   784	      for (var fii = 0; fii < finalized.length; fii++) {
   785	        HI.safeDefineDataProperty(finalIdSet, finalized[fii].priorityId, true);
   786	      }
   787	      var built = [];
   788	      for (var bi2 = 0; bi2 < finalized.length; bi2++) {
   789	        var cur = finalized[bi2];
   790	        var blockingIds = [];
   791	        for (var bj = 0; bj < finalized.length; bj++) {
   792	          if (bj === bi2) continue;
   793	          var other = finalized[bj];
   794	          if (other.tier >= cur.tier) continue;
   795	          if (other.hyp.status === 'contradicted' || other.hyp.status === 'blocked') continue;
   796	          if (!_crAtLeast(other.hyp.credibility, 'Derived')) continue;
   797	          // Defensive referential check (Codex D4 R1 D4-R1-04 closure): prerequisite priorityId
   798	          // must exist in the final set.
   799	          if (!HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(finalIdSet, other.priorityId)) continue;
   800	          _arrPush(blockingIds, other.priorityId);
   801	        }
   802	        // _buildPriorityEntry would re-derive kind for setup downgrade — pass the already-finalized
   803	        // values via a wrapper hypothesis that pre-sets the kind/tier intent. To keep
   804	        // _buildPriorityEntry stable, we still call it; the kind it computes will MATCH cur.kind
   805	        // (since we applied the same downgrade logic above), and the priorityId it computes will
   806	        // MATCH cur.priorityId (since both use _priorityId(hypothesisId, kind, schemaVersion)).
   807	        var entry = _buildPriorityEntry(cur.hyp, cur.tier, blockingIds, PRIORITY_SET_SCHEMA_VERSION);
   808	        _arrPush(built, entry);
   809	      }
   810	
   811	      if (!_intrinsicsIntact()) {
   812	        return _buildIntrinsicTamperBlock('intrinsic-tampering detected mid-build');
   813	      }
   814	
   815	      // ---- Step 5 — sort by ladder ----
   816	      // Primary sort: tier ascending; secondary: confidence.score descending; tertiary: hypothesisId
   817	      var sortedPriorities = HI.safeArraySlice(built);
   818	      HI.safeArraySort(sortedPriorities, function (a, b) {
   819	        if (a.tier !== b.tier) return a.tier - b.tier;
   820	        if (a.confidence.score !== b.confidence.score) return b.confidence.score - a.confidence.score;
   821	        return _strcmp(a.hypothesisId, b.hypothesisId);
   822	      });
   823	
   824	      if (sortedPriorities.length > PRIORITY_COUNT_CAP) {
   825	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'priority count exceeds PRIORITY_COUNT_CAP=' + PRIORITY_COUNT_CAP });
     1	/**
     2	 * renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js — R3.0D D3 · Hypothesis Engine (PRODUCTION).
     3	 *
     4	 * Authoritative entry: buildHypothesisSet(input, opts)
     5	 *
     6	 * Hard contract (SKYLINE 2026-06-29 R3.0 Continuous Resume Directive §5):
     7	 *   • Input is a D2 AUTHORITATIVE Evidence Graph snapshot ONLY (deep-frozen, content-hashed).
     8	 *     The engine recomputes the graphId from the snapshot's own (caseAssociation, nodes, edges,
     9	 *     correlationGroups) and rejects the call if the recomputation does not match the asserted
    10	 *     graphId. Caller-fabricated objects cannot survive this check unless the caller also
    11	 *     reproduces the D2 hash exactly — and even if they do, the rest of the contract still gates
    12	 *     freshness, case/session scope, and structural integrity.
    13	 *   • No LLM. No runtime config. No filesystem. Pure deterministic transformation.
    14	 *   • Closed rule registry — no string-eval, no Function constructor, no caller-supplied rule.
    15	 *   • Output is a deep-frozen Hypothesis Set envelope. Every nested array/object frozen.
    16	 *   • Confidence is computed by D3 from evidence weighting; caller-supplied confidence is
    17	 *     rejected. State enum {not_computed, insufficient_evidence, low, moderate, high}; numeric
    18	 *     score is bounded integer 0..100 derived deterministically from independent supporting
    19	 *     groups + aggregate credibility + contradictions. Score is auxiliary; state is the
    20	 *     semantic primary. Freshness is used SOLELY as an eligibility / staleness gate (stale
    21	 *     nodes are rejected at authority verification) — it does NOT contribute to the
    22	 *     confidence score (Codex D3 R5 D3-R5-03 closure).
    23	 *   • All ambient intrinsic calls go through R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics (HI). Mutation-tested
    24	 *     coverage proves regressions are caught. The validation path NEVER invokes
    25	 *     attacker-replaced ambient intrinsics — that is the primary defence; the four integrity
    26	 *     checkpoints (Step 0 entry, Step 1.5 post-envelope, Step 3.5 per-rule, Step 9.6 post-clock)
    27	 *     are defence-in-depth.
    28	 *   • Output never contains raw telemetry samples, filesystem paths, source filenames,
    29	 *     usernames, machine IDs, internal database handles, stack traces, or Electron private
    30	 *     paths. All caps enforce fail-closed (no partial output on failure).
    31	 *
    32	 * UMD: Node require / Electron renderer global (R3_0D_HypothesisEngine).
    33	 */
    34	(function (root) {
    35	  'use strict';
    36	
    37	  // ---------- Module-init capture: hardened intrinsics ------------------------------------------
    38	  // UMD imports use literal-string specifiers below so the no-consumer scanner can statically
    39	  // discover all dependencies.
    40	  var HI = null, RC = null, CR = null, HC = null, EN = null, SI = null;
    41	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
    42	    try { HI = require('../../contracts/r3.0d/hardened-intrinsics.js'); } catch (e) { HI = null; }
    43	    try { RC = require('../../contracts/r3.0d/reason-codes.js'); } catch (e) { RC = null; }
    44	    try { CR = require('../../contracts/r3.0d/credibility-contract.js'); } catch (e) { CR = null; }
    45	    try { HC = require('../../contracts/r3.0d/hypothesis-contract.js'); } catch (e) { HC = null; }
    46	    try { EN = require('../../contracts/r3.0d/evidence-node-contract.js'); } catch (e) { EN = null; }
    47	    try { SI = require('../../contracts/r3.0d/source-identity-contract.js'); } catch (e) { SI = null; }
    48	  }
    49	  if (HI === null && typeof R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics !== 'undefined') HI = R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics;
    50	  if (RC === null && typeof R3_0D_ReasonCodes !== 'undefined') RC = R3_0D_ReasonCodes;
    51	  if (CR === null && typeof R3_0D_CredibilityContract !== 'undefined') CR = R3_0D_CredibilityContract;
    52	  if (HC === null && typeof R3_0D_HypothesisContract !== 'undefined') HC = R3_0D_HypothesisContract;
    53	  if (EN === null && typeof R3_0D_EvidenceNodeContract !== 'undefined') EN = R3_0D_EvidenceNodeContract;
    54	  if (SI === null && typeof R3_0D_SourceIdentityContract !== 'undefined') SI = R3_0D_SourceIdentityContract;
    55	
    56	  if (!HI || !RC || !CR || !HC || !EN || !SI) {
    57	    throw new Error('r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js: missing one or more required R3.0D contracts');
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  var CODES = RC.REASON_CODES;
    61	
    62	  // ---------- Module-init capture: Object.isFrozen + Date.parse + Object.is -------------------
    63	  // These primitives are not in the HI surface; we capture them at module init so callers
    64	  // cannot defeat security checks by rebinding the ambient names. NOTE (Codex D3 R2 RN-07
    65	  // narrowed claim): "module init" here means after the contract dependencies have loaded
    66	  // (HI, RC, ...) — those loads themselves trust the module loader. If the module loader is
    67	  // compromised these captures cannot help, but that is out-of-scope (the entire R3.0D
    68	  // contract assumes module-init trust).
    69	  var _CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS_FROZEN = Object.isFrozen;
    70	  var _CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS = Object.is;
    71	  var _CAPTURED_DATE_PARSE = Date.parse;
    72	  function _isFrozenSafe(v) {
    73	    try { return _CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS_FROZEN(v) === true; }
    74	    catch (e) { return false; }
    75	  }
    76	  function _exactlyEqual(a, b) {
    77	    try { return _CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS(a, b) === true; }
    78	    catch (e) { return false; }
    79	  }
    80	  function _isoToMs(s) {
    81	    try {
    82	      if (typeof s !== 'string' || s.length === 0) return null;
    83	      var n = _CAPTURED_DATE_PARSE(s);
    84	      if (typeof n !== 'number' || n !== n /* NaN check */) return null;
    85	      return n;
    86	    } catch (e) { return null; }
    87	  }
    88	
    89	  // ---------- Module-init intrinsic snapshot for the post-load guard -----------------------------
    90	  // Step 0 entry guard recomputes these against the live HI api and rejects the call if the
    91	  // module-init capture was poisoned at module load time. JS is single-threaded so a guard pass
    92	  // here means no further mid-call rebinding of THESE references can occur (HI internals are
    93	  // closure-private and themselves cannot be rebound).
    94	  var _HI_API_FROZEN_AT_LOAD = (function () {
    95	    try { return Object.isFrozen(HI); } catch (e) { return false; }
    96	  })();
    97	  var _HI_HAS = (function () {
    98	    var required = [
    99	      'safeOwnKeys', 'safeKeys', 'safeGetOwnDescriptor', 'safeHasOwn',
   100	      'safeArrayPush', 'safeArrayIndexOf', 'safeArrayForEach', 'safeArrayMap',
   101	      'safeArraySlice', 'safeArraySort', 'safeStringSlice', 'safeStringCharCodeAt',
   102	      'safeStringCoerce', 'safeStringToLowerCase', 'safeIsArray', 'safeIsPlainShape',
   103	      'safeNumberIsInteger', 'safeNumberIsFinite', 'safeMathFloor', 'safeRegExpTest',
   104	      'safeUtf8ByteLength', 'safeStructuredClone', 'safeObjectCreateNull',
   105	      'safeObjectAssign', 'safeDefineDataProperty', 'safeGetPrototypeOf',
   106	      'safeGetOwnPropertyNames', 'deepOriginalShapeAudit', 'deepFreeze', 'stableStringify',
   107	    ];
   108	    var ok = true;
   109	    for (var i = 0; i < required.length; i++) {
   110	      if (typeof HI[required[i]] !== 'function') { ok = false; break; }
   111	    }
   112	    return ok;
   113	  })();
   114	
   115	  function _intrinsicsIntact() {
   116	    if (!_HI_API_FROZEN_AT_LOAD) return false;
   117	    if (!_HI_HAS) return false;
   118	    // The HI api itself MUST still be frozen at call time. We use the captured Object.isFrozen
   119	    // (Codex D3 R1 RN-08 closure) so caller cannot defeat this check by rebinding ambient.
   120	    if (!_isFrozenSafe(HI)) return false;
   121	    // Codex D3 R1 RN-10 closure: narrow guard claim. This routine ONLY verifies HI surface
   122	    // integrity (and our captured Object.isFrozen reference is sound, since we capture it before
   123	    // any caller code runs). Validation-path security relies on the HI captured wrappers — the
   124	    // guard is defence-in-depth on the HI surface itself, not a general intrinsic-rebinding
   125	    // detector.
      catch (eFinalBytes) { finalEnvBytes = Infinity; }
      if (finalEnvBytes > ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP) {
        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'final envelope ' + finalEnvBytes + ' bytes exceeds ' + ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP });
      }

      // ---- Step 9.6 — post-freeze final intrinsic check ----
      if (!_intrinsicsIntact()) {
        return _buildIntrinsicTamperBlock('intrinsic-tampering detected post-freeze');
      }

      return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: true, hypothesisSet: hypothesisSet });

    } catch (eOuter) {
      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'buildHypothesisSet threw on hostile input' });
    }
  }

  function _incReason(bag, code) {
    var d = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(bag, code);
    var current = (d && typeof d.value === 'number') ? d.value : 0;
    HI.safeDefineDataProperty(bag, code, current + 1);
  }

  // ---------- Public API ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  var api = {
    SERVICE_VERSION: SERVICE_VERSION,
    HYPOTHESIS_SET_SCHEMA_VERSION: HYPOTHESIS_SET_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    CONFIDENCE_STATES: CONFIDENCE_STATES,
    HYPOTHESIS_STATUSES: HYPOTHESIS_STATUSES,
    RULE_REGISTRY: RULE_REGISTRY,
    HYPOTHESIS_COUNT_CAP: HYPOTHESIS_COUNT_CAP,
    ALT_EXPLANATION_COUNT_CAP: ALT_EXPLANATION_COUNT_CAP,
    VALIDATION_ACTION_COUNT_CAP: VALIDATION_ACTION_COUNT_CAP,
    CANNOT_CONCLUDE_CAP: CANNOT_CONCLUDE_CAP,
    LIMITATIONS_CAP: LIMITATIONS_CAP,
    ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP: ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP,
    buildHypothesisSet: buildHypothesisSet,
  };
  // Freeze the api object so callers cannot replace exports. Uses HI.deepFreeze (captured
  // intrinsic) for consistency with every other freeze in this module — no ambient calls.
  try { HI.deepFreeze(api); } catch (e) { /* swallow — best-effort */ }

  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
  if (root) root.R3_0D_HypothesisEngine = api;
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);

   441	// ---------- Section N — Forbidden behaviors per directive §12 -----------------------
   442	console.log('Section N — forbidden behaviors');
   443	(function () {
   444	  // N1: Heuristic-only hypothesis cannot be primary
   445	  var n = _baseNode({ credibility: 'heuristic', limitations: ['LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY'] });

   117	    if (p.tier < prevTier) { sortOk = false; break; }

   187	// ---------- Section D — Scope ----------------------------------------------------------
   188	console.log('Section D — scope');
   189	(function () {
   190	  var hs = _buildHypothesisSet([_baseNode({})]);
   419	      if (!_isPlainObject(h.confidence)) {
   420	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   421	      }
   422	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(CONFIDENCE_STATE_ALLOWED, h.confidence.state) === -1) {
   423	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   424	      }
   425	      if (typeof h.confidence.score !== 'number'
   426	          || !_isInteger(h.confidence.score)
   427	          || h.confidence.score < 0
   428	          || h.confidence.score > 100) {
   429	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   430	      }
   431	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(HYPOTHESIS_STATUS_ALLOWED, h.status) === -1) {
   432	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   433	      }
   434	      // Cross-field: status='contradicted' ⟹ contradictingEvidenceIds.length > 0
   435	      if (h.status === 'contradicted' && h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length === 0) {
   436	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   437	      }
   438	      // status='supported' ⟹ supportingEvidenceIds.length > 0
   439	      if (h.status === 'supported' && h.supportingEvidenceIds.length === 0) {
   440	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   441	      }
   442	      if (!_isPlainArray(h.limitations)) {
   443	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   444	      }
   445	      if (h.limitations.length > 32) {
   446	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   447	      }
   448	      for (var lhi = 0; lhi < h.limitations.length; lhi++) {
   449	        if (!RC.isReasonCode(h.limitations[lhi])) {
   450	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID] };
   451	        }
   452	      }
   453	    }
   454	
   455	    // Recompute hypothesisSetId — independent authority verification.
   456	    var recomputed;
   457	    try { recomputed = _recomputeHypothesisSetId(hs); }
   458	    catch (e) { return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] }; }
   459	    if (recomputed !== hs.hypothesisSetId) {
   460	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   461	    }
   462	
   463	    // Freshness — D3 already enforced node freshness, but D4 ALSO checks the snapshot's
   464	    // createdAt is not stale (so a snapshot kept indefinitely cannot drive priorities).
   465	    // hs.createdAt may be null (referenceNowMs-supplied path) — in that case D4 trusts the
   466	    // D3 freshness gate already passed and uses refNowMs as effective now.

     1	/**
     2	 * contracts/r3.0d/hypothesis-contract.js — R3.0D D1 · Contract Foundation (NON-PRODUCTION).
     3	 *
     4	 * Defines Hypothesis + Contradiction + AlternativeExplanation + CannotConclude + Limitation +
     5	 * ValidationAction SHAPES. The D1 contract layer enforces the structural reasoning discipline that
     6	 * directive §8 mandates:
     7	 *   • supportingEvidenceIds      — required
     8	 *   • contradictingEvidenceIds   — required
     9	 *   • alternativeExplanationIds  — required
    10	 *   • cannotConcludeReasonCodes  — required
    11	 *   • limitations                — required
    12	 *   • validationActionIds        — required
    13	 * A Hypothesis that omits ANY of these (even with an empty array) → HYPOTHESIS_INVALID. Free-text
    14	 * causal authority is rejected as a structural posture, not a style preference. The hypothesis MUST
    15	 * carry an i18nKey for any narrative; free-form prose at the authority layer is forbidden.
    16	 *
    17	 * Causal overclaim rejection (directive §8 "禁止輸出"):
    18	 *   • exact_cause, driver_fault, setup_caused_loss, guaranteed_fix, professional_diagnosis,
    19	 *     fastest_setup, theoretical_best — any of these strings appearing in i18nKey OR any param
    20	 *     value triggers HYPOTHESIS_CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM. D1 catches the lexical surface; D3 adds semantic
    21	 *     analysis.
    22	 *
    23	 * Confidence: D1 forbids any numeric confidence on a Hypothesis. The hypothesis's confidence field
    24	 * MUST be { state: 'unresolved' | 'not_computed' } only — D3 produces the numeric value.
    25	 *
    26	 * UMD: Node require / Electron renderer global (R3_0D_HypothesisContract).
    27	 */

    32	  var RC = _req('./reason-codes.js', typeof R3_0D_ReasonCodes !== 'undefined' ? R3_0D_ReasonCodes : undefined);

    84	  var HYPOTHESIS_ID_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$/;

    90	  var CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM_TERMS = Object.freeze([

   100	  function _isPlain(v) { if (v == null || typeof v !== 'object' || Array.isArray(v)) return false; try { var p = Object.getPrototypeOf(v); return p === Object.prototype || p === null; } catch (e) { return false; } }
   101	  function _nonEmptyStr(v) { return typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0; }
   102	  // Codex D1 R1 Finding RN-01 closure.
   103	  function _hasOnlyAllowedKeys(o, allowed) { var keys; try { keys = Reflect.ownKeys(o); } catch (e) { return false; } for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) { var k = keys[i]; if (typeof k === 'symbol') return false; if (allowed.indexOf(k) === -1) return false; } return true; }
   104	  function _isFiniteNum(v) { return typeof v === 'number' && v === v && v !== Infinity && v !== -Infinity; }
   105	  function _utf8Bytes(s) { try { return (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') ? new TextEncoder().encode(s).length : Buffer.byteLength(s, 'utf8'); } catch (e) { return (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length * 4 : 0; } }
   106	  // Codex D1 R1 Finding RN-05 closure: ID-array elements MUST also pass the byte cap and the
   107	  // grammar regex (same as the parent id). Otherwise an arbitrarily-long string can pad an id slot
   108	  // before envelope validation. _isIdArray therefore takes the same id grammar + byte cap as the
   109	  // owning shape.
   110	  function _isIdArray(v, cap) {
   111	    if (!Array.isArray(v) || v.length > cap) return false;
   112	    for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) {
   113	      var e = v[i];
   114	      if (typeof e !== 'string' || e.length === 0) return false;
   115	      if (HYPOTHESIS_ID_FORBIDDEN_RE.test(e) || !HYPOTHESIS_ID_RE.test(e)) return false;
   116	      if (_utf8Bytes(e) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) return false;
   117	    }
   118	    return true;
   119	  }
   120	  // Codex D1 R2 Finding RN-08 closure: centralized normalization covering ALL Unicode dash characters.
   121	  // Previous regex only covered U+2010–U+2015 (en-dash family) but missed U+2212 MINUS SIGN, U+FF0D
   122	  // FULLWIDTH HYPHEN-MINUS, and the general \p{Pd} Dash_Punctuation category. _normalizeForOverclaim
   123	  // is exported on the api so recommendation-contract + engineer-brief-contract use the SAME canonical
   124	  // normalizer — no duplicated regex.
   125	  // Dash character class: U+002D (-) ASCII hyphen-minus, U+2010–U+2015 (hyphen / non-breaking hyphen /
   126	  // figure dash / en dash / em dash / horizontal bar), U+2212 (minus sign), U+FE58 (small em dash),
   127	  // U+FE63 (small hyphen-minus), U+FF0D (fullwidth hyphen-minus), U+058A (Armenian hyphen),
   128	  // U+05BE (Hebrew maqaf), U+1806 (Mongolian todo soft hyphen), U+1400 (Canadian hyphen), U+2E17
   129	  // (double oblique hyphen), U+2E1A (hyphen with diaeresis), U+2E3A/B (two/three-em dash), U+2E40
   130	  // (double hyphen), U+30A0 (Katakana-Hiragana double hyphen).
   131	  // Explicit Unicode escapes to remove any ambiguity in the regex parser. Includes ASCII hyphen-minus
   132	  // (U+002D), the U+2010..U+2015 dash family (HYPHEN / NON-BREAKING / FIGURE / EN / EM / HORIZONTAL),
   133	  // U+2212 MINUS SIGN, U+FF0D FULLWIDTH HYPHEN-MINUS, U+FE58/63 SMALL EM/HYPHEN, U+058A Armenian,
   134	  // U+05BE Hebrew MAQAF, U+1806 Mongolian SOFT HYPHEN, U+1400 Canadian, U+2E17 DOUBLE OBLIQUE,
   135	  // U+2E1A HYPHEN-DIAERESIS, U+2E3A/B TWO/THREE EM DASH, U+2E40 DOUBLE HYPHEN, U+30A0 Kana double.
   136	  var DASH_OR_WS_RE = /[\s-‐‑‒–—―−－﹘﹣֊־᠆᐀⸗⸚⸺⸻⹀゠]+/g;
   137	  function _normalizeForOverclaim(s) {
   138	    if (typeof s !== 'string') return '';
   139	    return s.toLowerCase().replace(DASH_OR_WS_RE, '_');
   140	  }
   141	  function _hasCausalOverclaim(s) {
   142	    if (typeof s !== 'string') return false;
   143	    var normalized = _normalizeForOverclaim(s);
   144	    for (var i = 0; i < CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM_TERMS.length; i++) {
   145	      var t = _normalizeForOverclaim(CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM_TERMS[i]);
   146	      if (normalized.indexOf(t) !== -1) return true;
   147	    }
   148	    return false;
   149	  }

   151	  /**
   152	   * validateParamsShape(p) — closed key plain object, values ∈ { finite number, boolean, null, short
   153	   * string }. Same posture as evidence-node observation params.
   154	   */
   155	  function validateParamsShape(p) {
   156	    // RN-02 closure: outer try/catch. RN-01 closure: Reflect.ownKeys rejects Symbol-keyed extras.
   157	    try {
   158	      if (p === null || p === undefined) return Object.freeze({ valid: true });
   159	      if (!_isPlain(p)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]);
   160	      var keys; try { keys = Reflect.ownKeys(p); } catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]); }
   161	      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
   162	        var k = keys[i];
   163	        if (typeof k === 'symbol') return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   164	        var v = p[k];
   165	        if (v === null || typeof v === 'boolean') continue;
   166	        if (typeof v === 'number') { if (!_isFiniteNum(v)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.NUMERIC_INVALID]); continue; }
   167	        if (typeof v === 'string') {
   168	          if (_utf8Bytes(v) > PARAMS_VALUE_BYTE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED]);
   169	          if (_hasCausalOverclaim(v)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM]);
   170	          continue;
   171	        }
   172	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]);
   173	      }
   174	      return Object.freeze({ valid: true });
   175	    } catch (e) {
   176	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'params validator threw on hostile input' });
   177	    }
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  /**
   181	   * validateAlternativeExplanationShape(a) — closed-key plain object.
   182	   */
   183	  function validateAlternativeExplanationShape(a) {
   184	    try {
   185	    if (!_isPlain(a)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_ALTERNATIVE_INVALID]);
   186	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(a, ALTERNATIVE_EXPLANATION_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_ALTERNATIVE_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   187	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(a.alternativeId) || HYPOTHESIS_ID_FORBIDDEN_RE.test(a.alternativeId) || !HYPOTHESIS_ID_RE.test(a.alternativeId)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_ALTERNATIVE_INVALID]);
   188	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(a.i18nKey)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_ALTERNATIVE_INVALID]);
   189	    if (_utf8Bytes(a.i18nKey) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED]);
   190	    if (_hasCausalOverclaim(a.i18nKey)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM]);
   191	    var pc = validateParamsShape('params' in a ? a.params : null);
   192	    if (pc.valid !== true) return pc;
   193	    if ('supportingEvidenceIds' in a) {
   194	      if (!_isIdArray(a.supportingEvidenceIds, ID_ARRAY_CAP)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_ALTERNATIVE_INVALID, CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED]);
   195	    }
   196	    return Object.freeze({ valid: true });
   197	    } catch (e) {
   198	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_ALTERNATIVE_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'alternative validator threw on hostile input' });
   199	    }
   200	  }

   202	  /**
   203	   * validateValidationActionShape(a) — closed-key plain object with kind ∈ VALIDATION_ACTION_KIND_ALLOWED.
   204	   */
   205	  function validateValidationActionShape(a) {
   206	    try {
   207	    if (!_isPlain(a)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.VALIDATION_ACTION_INVALID]);
   208	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(a, VALIDATION_ACTION_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.VALIDATION_ACTION_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   209	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(a.actionId) || HYPOTHESIS_ID_FORBIDDEN_RE.test(a.actionId) || !HYPOTHESIS_ID_RE.test(a.actionId)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.VALIDATION_ACTION_INVALID]);
   210	    if (VALIDATION_ACTION_KIND_ALLOWED.indexOf(a.kind) === -1) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.VALIDATION_ACTION_UNKNOWN_KIND]);
   211	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(a.i18nKey)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.VALIDATION_ACTION_INVALID]);
   212	    if (_utf8Bytes(a.i18nKey) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED]);
   213	    if (_hasCausalOverclaim(a.i18nKey)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM]);
   214	    if ('params' in a) { var pc = validateParamsShape(a.params); if (pc.valid !== true) return pc; }
   215	    if ('requiresControlledVariables' in a) {
   216	      if (a.requiresControlledVariables !== true && a.requiresControlledVariables !== false) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.VALIDATION_ACTION_INVALID]);
   217	    }
   218	    if ('expectedObservationI18nKey' in a && a.expectedObservationI18nKey !== null) {
   219	      if (!_nonEmptyStr(a.expectedObservationI18nKey)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.VALIDATION_ACTION_INVALID]);
   220	      if (_utf8Bytes(a.expectedObservationI18nKey) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED]);
   221	      if (_hasCausalOverclaim(a.expectedObservationI18nKey)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM]);
   222	    }
   223	    return Object.freeze({ valid: true });
   224	    } catch (e) {
   225	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.VALIDATION_ACTION_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'validation-action validator threw on hostile input' });
   226	    }
   227	  }

   229	  /**
   230	   * validateHypothesisShape(h) — D1 STRUCTURAL gate. Composes credibility, source-identity, params,
   231	   * alternative-explanation, validation-action validators. Returns { valid:true } or buildBlockedResult.
   232	   */
   233	  function validateHypothesisShape(hIn) {
   234	    // Codex D1 R2 RN-06 Proxy-rejection input clone.
   235	    try {
   236	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(hIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'hypothesis prototype is not Object.prototype or null' });
   237	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(hIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'hypothesis carries Symbol-keyed or non-enumerable own property' });
   238	    if (RC.hasNonPlainNestedObject(hIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'hypothesis contains a nested non-plain object (class instance laundered through identity / confidence / nested entries)' });
   239	    var h = RC.toCleanCopy(hIn);
   240	    if (!_isPlain(h)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID], { detail: 'hypothesis not plain object (or proxy/non-cloneable rejected)' });
   241	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(h, HYPOTHESIS_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   242	    var reasons = [];
   243	
   244	    if (!Number.isInteger(h.schemaVersion)) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID);
   245	    else if (h.schemaVersion > SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION) reasons.push(CODES.UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA);
   246	    else if (h.schemaVersion < 1) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID);
   247	
   248	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(h.hypothesisId)) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID);
   249	    else if (HYPOTHESIS_ID_FORBIDDEN_RE.test(h.hypothesisId) || !HYPOTHESIS_ID_RE.test(h.hypothesisId)) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID);
   250	
   251	    if (HYPOTHESIS_CATEGORIES.indexOf(h.category) === -1) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CATEGORY_UNKNOWN);
   252	
   253	    var idCheck = SI.validateSourceIdentity(h.identity);
   254	    if (idCheck.valid !== true) reasons.push(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID);
   255	
   256	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(h.i18nKey)) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID);
   257	    else {
   258	      if (_utf8Bytes(h.i18nKey) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) reasons.push(CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED);
   259	      if (_hasCausalOverclaim(h.i18nKey)) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CAUSAL_OVERCLAIM);
   260	    }
   261	
   262	    var pc = validateParamsShape('params' in h ? h.params : null);
   263	    if (pc.valid !== true) reasons.push.apply(reasons, pc.reasonCodes || [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID]);
   264	
   265	    // Credibility — uses CONCLUSION_CREDIBILITY ladder (not EVIDENCE_CREDIBILITY). A heuristic
   266	    // conclusion MUST declare LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY.
   267	    if (CR.CONCLUSION_CREDIBILITY.indexOf(h.credibility) === -1) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID);
   268	
   269	    // Confidence — caller cannot supply numeric.
   270	    var cfCheck = CR.validateConfidenceShape(h.confidence);
   271	    if (cfCheck.valid !== true) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CONFIDENCE_FORBIDDEN);
   272	
   273	    // Each evidence/alt/validation array MUST be present (even if empty). Directive §8: structured
   274	    // reasoning output requires all six slots — omission is itself a hypothesis-structure violation.
   275	    if (!_isIdArray(h.supportingEvidenceIds, ID_ARRAY_CAP)) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_EVIDENCE_LINK_INVALID);
   276	    if (!_isIdArray(h.contradictingEvidenceIds, ID_ARRAY_CAP)) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_CONTRADICTION_INVALID);
   277	    // Codex D1 R2 Finding RN-09 closure: alternativeExplanationIds was the one remaining id-array that
   278	    // bypassed _isIdArray's grammar + byte-cap check. Route through _isIdArray with ALTERNATIVE_ARRAY_CAP.
   279	    if (!_isIdArray(h.alternativeExplanationIds, ALTERNATIVE_ARRAY_CAP)) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_ALTERNATIVE_INVALID);
   280	    if (!Array.isArray(h.cannotConcludeReasonCodes) || h.cannotConcludeReasonCodes.length > ID_ARRAY_CAP) reasons.push(CODES.HYPOTHESIS_INVALID);
279:        i18nKey: n.observation.i18nKey,
284:        category: n.category,
768:  var RULE_REGISTRY = HI.deepFreeze([
770:      ruleId: 'rule_dq_channel_missing',
772:      category: 'data_quality',
779:      minConclusionCredibility: 'Heuristic',
780:      i18nKey: 'r3.0d.hypothesis.data_quality.channel_missing',
783:        i18nKey: 'r3.0d.validation.fix_data_quality.channel_missing',
788:        HI.deepFreeze({ altSuffix: 'logger_misconfig', i18nKey: 'r3.0d.alt.logger_misconfig' }),
789:        HI.deepFreeze({ altSuffix: 'sensor_failure', i18nKey: 'r3.0d.alt.sensor_failure' }),
794:      ruleId: 'rule_dq_channel_partial',
796:      category: 'data_quality',

   486	  // ---------- Eligibility checks (per directive §12 FORBIDDEN list) ----------------------------
   487	  /**
   488	   * A hypothesis is eligible to become PRIMARY only if:
   489	   *   - credibility >= Derived (not Heuristic / Unavailable)
   490	   *   - status NOT 'contradicted' or 'blocked'
   491	   *   - confidence.state ∈ {moderate, high}
   492	   * setup_experiment additionally requires: zero contradicting evidence + confidence high
   493	   * (per directive: "unsupported setup actions" forbidden).
   494	   */
   495	  function _eligibleAsPrimary(h) {
   496	    if (!_crAtLeast(h.credibility, 'Derived')) return false;
   497	    if (h.status === 'contradicted' || h.status === 'blocked') return false;
   498	    if (h.confidence.state !== 'moderate' && h.confidence.state !== 'high') return false;
   499	    return true;
   500	  }
   501	  function _eligibleAsSetupExperiment(h) {
   502	    if (h.contradictingEvidenceIds && h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length > 0) return false;
   503	    if (h.confidence.state !== 'high') return false;
   504	    if (!_crAtLeast(h.credibility, 'Derived')) return false;
   505	    return true;
   506	  }

   508	  // ---------- Priority ID generators -----------------------------------------------------------
   509	  function _priorityId(hypothesisId, kind, schemaVersion) {
   510	    var hash = _hashFNV64Hex('priorityid|v' + schemaVersion + '|' + hypothesisId + '|' + kind);
   511	    return 'pri_' + hash;
   512	  }
   513	  function _prioritySetId(sourceHsetId, priorities, schemaVersion) {
   514	    var hashMaterial = {
   515	      v: schemaVersion,
   516	      hsetId: sourceHsetId,
   517	      priIds: HI.safeArrayMap(priorities, function (p) { return p.priorityId; }),
   518	    };
   519	    HI.safeArraySort(hashMaterial.priIds, _strcmp);
   520	    return 'priset_' + _hashFNV64Hex('prioritysetid|v' + schemaVersion + '|' + HI.stableStringify(hashMaterial));
   521	  }

   523	  // ---------- Build one priority entry ---------------------------------------------------------
   524	  function _buildPriorityEntry(h, tier, blockingIds, schemaVersion) {
   525	    var kind = _kindForCategory(h.category);
   526	    // For setup_experiment, additional eligibility gate
   527	    if (kind === 'setup_experiment' && !_eligibleAsSetupExperiment(h)) {
   528	      // Downgrade to controlled_repeat_lap (still actionable, but doesn't claim setup causation)
   529	      kind = 'controlled_repeat_lap';
   530	      tier = PRIORITY_KIND_TIER[kind];
   531	    }
   532	    if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(PRIORITY_KIND_ALLOWED, kind) === -1) {
   533	      kind = 'no_action_required';
   534	      tier = PRIORITY_KIND_TIER[kind];
   535	    }
   536	    var pid = _priorityId(h.hypothesisId, kind, schemaVersion);
   537	
   538	    // Limitations (carry forward from hypothesis + add a default if heuristic / contradicted)
   539	    var lims = HI.safeArraySlice(_isPlainArray(h.limitations) ? h.limitations : []);
   540	    if (!_crAtLeast(h.credibility, 'Derived')
   541	        && HI.safeArrayIndexOf(lims, CODES.LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY) === -1) {
   542	      _arrPush(lims, CODES.LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY);
   543	    }
   544	    if (h.contradictingEvidenceIds && h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length > 0
   545	        && HI.safeArrayIndexOf(lims, CODES.LIMITATION_NO_CONTROLLED_REPEAT) === -1) {
   546	      _arrPush(lims, CODES.LIMITATION_NO_CONTROLLED_REPEAT);
   547	    }
   548	    HI.safeArraySort(lims, _strcmp);
   549	    if (lims.length > LIMITATIONS_PER_PRIORITY_CAP) {
   550	      var trimmed = [];
   551	      for (var li = 0; li < LIMITATIONS_PER_PRIORITY_CAP; li++) _arrPush(trimmed, lims[li]);
   552	      lims = trimmed;
   553	    }
   554	
   555	    var sortedBlocking = HI.safeArraySlice(blockingIds);
   556	    HI.safeArraySort(sortedBlocking, _strcmp);
   557	    if (sortedBlocking.length > PREREQ_ID_PER_PRIORITY_CAP) {
   558	      var blkTrimmed = [];
   559	      for (var bi = 0; bi < PREREQ_ID_PER_PRIORITY_CAP; bi++) _arrPush(blkTrimmed, sortedBlocking[bi]);
   560	      sortedBlocking = blkTrimmed;
   561	    }
   562	
   563	    // i18nKey for the priority itself, sourced deterministically from the kind.
   564	    var i18nKey = 'r3.0d.priority.' + kind;
   565	    var whyNowI18nKey = 'r3.0d.priority.why_now.' + kind;
   566	    var expectedObsKey = 'r3.0d.priority.expected.' + kind;
   567	    var stopKey = 'r3.0d.priority.stop.' + kind;
   568	    var rollbackKey = 'r3.0d.priority.rollback.' + kind;
   569	
   570	    return HI.deepFreeze({
   571	      priorityId: pid,
   572	      hypothesisId: h.hypothesisId,
   573	      rank: 0,  // assigned post-sort
   574	      tier: tier,
   575	      kind: kind,
   576	      category: h.category,
   577	      i18nKey: i18nKey,
   578	      whyNowI18nKey: whyNowI18nKey,
   579	      blockingPrerequisiteIds: HI.deepFreeze(sortedBlocking),
   580	      expectedObservationI18nKey: expectedObsKey,
   581	      stopConditionI18nKey: stopKey,
   582	      rollbackConditionI18nKey: rollbackKey,
   583	      credibility: h.credibility,
   584	      confidence: HI.deepFreeze({ state: h.confidence.state, score: h.confidence.score }),
   585	      eligibleAsPrimary: _eligibleAsPrimary(h),
   586	      contradictionCount: h.contradictingEvidenceIds ? h.contradictingEvidenceIds.length : 0,
   587	      limitations: HI.deepFreeze(lims),
   588	      provenance: HI.deepFreeze({
   589	        sourceHypothesisId: h.hypothesisId,
   590	        sourceCategory: h.category,
   760	    return { valid: true, graph: HI.deepFreeze(g) };
   761	  }

  1193	      contradictingIds.length,
  1194	      supportingIds.length,
  1195	      aggCred,
  1196	      rule
  1197	    );

  1200	
  1201	    var limitations = _aggregateLimitations(matched, rule);
  1202

  1204	      ruleId: rule.ruleId,
  1205	      ruleVersion: rule.ruleVersion,
  1206	      category: rule.category,
  1207	      i18nKey: rule.i18nKey,
  1208	      fired: true,

  1211	      contradictingIds: contradictingIds,

  1217	      limitations: limitations,

  1219	    };
  1220	  }

  1222	  // ---------- ID generators ---------------------------------------------------------------------
  1223	  function _hypothesisId(ruleId, supportingIds, contradictingIds, schemaVersion) {
  1224	    var idMaterial = {
  1225	      v: schemaVersion,
  1226	      ruleId: ruleId,
  1227	      supportingEvidenceIds: supportingIds,
  1228	      contradictingEvidenceIds: contradictingIds,
  1229	    };

  1231	    return 'hyp_' + hash;
  1232	  }

  1234	    var hash = _hashFNV64Hex('altid|v1|' + hypothesisId + '|' + suffix);
  1235	    return 'alt_' + hash;
  1236	  }

  1238	    var hash = _hashFNV64Hex('actionid|v1|' + hypothesisId + '|' + suffix);
  1239	    return 'act_' + hash;
  1240	  }

  1246	    };

  1248	    var hash = _hashFNV64Hex('hypothesissetid|v' + schemaVersion + '|' + HI.stableStringify(hashMaterial));
  1249	    return 'hset_' + hash;
  1250	  }

  1253	  function _buildHypothesisRecord(evalResult, schemaVersion) {

  1269	      }));
  1270	    }
  1271	    HI.safeArraySort(altIds, _strcmp);
  1272	    HI.safeArraySort(alts, function (x, y) { return _strcmp(x.alternativeId, y.alternativeId); });
  1273	
  1274	    // Build validation action
  1275	    var actId = _actionId(hid, rule.validationAction.kind);

  1282	      expectedObservationI18nKey: rule.validationAction.expectedObservationI18nKey,
  1283	      prerequisiteIds: HI.deepFreeze([]),
  1284	    })];

  1286

  1289	      ? _truncSortedCopy(evalResult.supportingIds, SUPPORT_ID_PER_HYPOTHESIS_CAP)
  1290	      : HI.safeArraySlice(evalResult.supportingIds);
  1291	    var contradictingCapped = evalResult.contradictingIds.length > CONTRADICT_ID_PER_HYPOTHESIS_CAP
  1292	      ? _truncSortedCopy(evalResult.contradictingIds, CONTRADICT_ID_PER_HYPOTHESIS_CAP)
  1293	      : HI.safeArraySlice(evalResult.contradictingIds);

  1300	      ruleId: rule.ruleId,
  1301	      ruleVersion: rule.ruleVersion,

  1315	      }),

  1321	        soloSupportCount: evalResult.supportingIds.length - evalResult.correlationGroupIds.length >= 0
  1322	          ? (evalResult.supportingIds.length - evalResult.correlationGroupIds.length)
  1323	          : 0,
  1324	        contradictionCount: evalResult.contradictingIds.length,
  1325	        supportCount: evalResult.supportingIds.length,
  1326	      }),
  1327	    });
  1328	    return { hypothesis: hyp, alts: alts, actions: actions };
